- **ai-engineering-maturity**: Framework for the 8 stages of AI engineering maturity (team and organization view). Use to assess adoption of AI tooling (Grok skills/agents/prompts, MCP, shared context, guardrails), diagnose drift or islands, plan advancement without skipping stages, and ensure AI amplifies good foundational practices rather than bad ones. - **ai-generate-image**: You are an AI 3D generation assistant. ([thomasmartinoa/AI_3Dpipeline](https://github.com/thomasmartinoa/AI_3Dpipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ai-generate-image.md))
- **ai-generate-text**: You are an AI 3D generation assistant. ([thomasmartinoa/AI_3Dpipeline](https://github.com/thomasmartinoa/AI_3Dpipeline/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ai-generate-text.md))
- **ai-governance**: Audit or build an enterprise AI governance program across all 6 layers. ([dmavridi6478/business](https://github.com/dmavridi6478/business/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ai-governance.md))
- **ai-governance**: Validates framework compliance, ownership boundaries, risk acceptance lifecycle, and manifest integrity for regulated environments. Trigger for 'are quality gates enforced', 'who owns this file', 'formally accept a known risk', 'pre-release compliance check', 'governance report for auditors'. Not for code quality; use /ai-verify instead. Not for security scanning; use /ai-security instead — this validates governance process, not code content. ([lucafregoso/luca-fregoso-personal-website-2026-astro](https://github.com/lucafregoso/luca-fregoso-personal-website-2026-astro/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/ai-governance.md))

- **ai-governance-check**: Review changed files for compliance with Balsm AI governance rules (AI_GOVERNANCE.md). ([balsm-health/Balsm-Core](https://github.com/balsm-health/Balsm-Core/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ai-governance-check.md) · ★5)
- **ai-guardrails-5-layers**: A complete framework for responsible AI deployment in organisations. Work bottom-up: System → Process → Operating Model → Governance → Culture. ([dmavridi6478/business](https://github.com/dmavridi6478/business/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ai-guardrails-5-layers.md))
- **ai-guides**: Audit and build the project's AI guide library — one source shared by Claude Code and opencode. ([teensee/dotfiles](https://github.com/teensee/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/ai-guides.md) · ★3)
- **ai-guides**: Audit and build the AI guide library shared by Claude Code and opencode ([teensee/dotfiles](https://github.com/teensee/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/opencode/commands/ai-guides.md) · ★3)
- **ai-handoff**: Use this when an agent finishes implementation or review. ([Sheng-wei-Tsai/gradland](https://github.com/Sheng-wei-Tsai/gradland/blob/HEAD/.agents/ai-workflow/commands/ai-handoff.md))
- **ai-heartbeat**: 这是 Claude Code 的 `/ai-heartbeat` slash command 入口。本文件只负责 OS 探测和引用执行合同。 ([iasiv5/ob-harness](https://github.com/iasiv5/ob-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ai-heartbeat.md) · ★4)
- **ai-help**: Emits the AI Agent Contract — a pretty-printed JSON document that fully describes every command, subcommand, flag, exit code, enum, and example exposed by the binary. ([FlavioCFOliveira/Groadmap](https://github.com/FlavioCFOliveira/Groadmap/blob/HEAD/DOCS/commands/ai-help.md) · ★2)

- **ai-history**: How much of this repo did Claude help write? Instant answer from git history — share of commits and lines added with a Claude trailer, by author and month.
